# Credit terms: preconditions rewritten as a scenario (26 Oct 2026)

QA lead, 26 Oct 2026: *"Preconditions should be like a scenario, easier for a manual QA tester to understand  to create in the account."* (Rule 117, thirteenth amendment; lesson L-10). Draft only: nothing written to TestRail, Jira, Slack or Google; nothing committed.

Generated by `scenario-preconditions-2026-10-26/work/make_report.py` from `before/proposals.json` (the rendered fields before the pass) and `authoring/proposals.json` (after).

## 1. Checks
- Cases: 344. Preconditions changed: 344. Unchanged: 0.
- Every other field byte-identical (title, section, refs, codes, automation marker, held flag, api flag, template, type, priority, custom_steps, custom_expected and the setup-file link above Preconditions): **YES, 0 cases differ**.
- `authoring/build.py`: PROBLEM lines 0, including the new gate (exactly one "Scenario:" line, first in Preconditions, at most 40 words, one sentence, no requirement code or placeholder, no "QA lead" / "ask your"; no precondition says who to ask or uses a set-aside line). Longest Scenario line: 25 words.
- Setup file (`CREDIT-TERMS-SETUP-FILE.md`, regenerated): every case's recipe repeats its Scenario line and every precondition word for word: **YES**.
- No fact lost: every placeholder, example name, work-order number, amount, term and day count in the old preconditions is still in the new ones: **YES**.
  - Same fact, new words: S1-25 "day 10" is now worded as in the QA lead's worked example: "due on the 10th of the following month".

## 2. What changed in the preconditions (every case)
- A "Scenario:" line opens every case's Preconditions (344 written by hand, one per case, in `authoring/scenarios.py`; rendered first by `ct_lib.render`).
- Who you are: "You are signed in as a user with the \"Admin\" role, working in {Location-A}." now reads "You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}." (the worked example's words).
- Starting screen on the Credit Terms page: "... is open on the Active tab." now reads "..., Active tab." (and the Inactive tab the same way).
- Design cases: the starting screen moved to the end, after the window size and the attached pictures (the order a tester sets things up).
- Targeted rewording (facts kept, order or example fixed): S1-25 (the worked example: two own terms first, then the default, then what is switched off); S4-10 (the term's example sat on the user-name line, before the term was named); S10-11 to S10-16 (examples name their placeholder; "For example: the request tool is Postman."); S10-14 (the new customer and the existing customer on separate lines); S12-09 (the customers in the example are named as the term's customers); DATA-05, DATA-06b, DATA-10, DATA-16 (examples name their placeholders); D-17 (it said both "COD is the shop's default" and "Due on Receipt is the default ... instead of COD"; now one line: "Due on Receipt is the default for new customers, not COD.").
- Cases whose first step opens the screen itself keep no separate starting-screen line (79 cases, as before): their first step already says where to go, so a starting-screen line would add nothing the tester needs.

| **S7-02** A vendor invoice is due by the vendor's term and recalculated on change | • You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, working in {Location-A}. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.<br>• Vendor {Vendor-A} holds the shop's own term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, and vendor {Vendor-B} holds Net 30. / For example: {Vendor-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Vendor A, {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Fourteen and {Vendor-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Vendor B.<br>• A vendor invoice {Vendor-invoice-A} for {Vendor-A}, not yet saved, is open in Parts, Vendors, Vendor Invoices. / For example: {Vendor-invoice-A} is a new vendor invoice for one part.<br>• If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done". | • Scenario: Your shop enters a bill from a vendor on a term of its own, then changes the bill's date and vendor.<br>• You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.<br>• Vendor {Vendor-A} holds the shop's own term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, and vendor {Vendor-B} holds Net 30. / For example: {Vendor-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Vendor A, {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Fourteen and {Vendor-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-02 Vendor B.<br>• A vendor invoice {Vendor-invoice-A} for {Vendor-A}, not yet saved, is open in Parts, Vendors, Vendor Invoices. / For example: {Vendor-invoice-A} is a new vendor invoice for one part.<br>• If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done". |

| **S7-04** The same invoice shows the same due date to users in other locations | • You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, working in {Location-A}. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.<br>• You are enrolled at {Location-A} and at {Location-B}, which is in another time zone. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 (Edmonton time) and {Location-B} is in Auckland.<br>• Customer {Customer-A} holds the shop's own term {Term-A}, due on day 10 of the following month, and has an invoice {Invoice-A} at {Location-A} dated Oct 5, 2026. / For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Customer and {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Tenth.<br>• A second user, {User-B}, without Settings → Finance, works in {Location-B}. / For example: {User-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Writer on the Service Advisor role.<br>• If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done". | • Scenario: Your shop works at two locations in different time zones, and users at both open the same invoice.<br>• You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.<br>• You are enrolled at {Location-A} and at {Location-B}, which is in another time zone. / For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 (Edmonton time) and {Location-B} is in Auckland.<br>• Customer {Customer-A} holds the shop's own term {Term-A}, due on day 10 of the following month, and has an invoice {Invoice-A} at {Location-A} dated Oct 5, 2026. / For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Customer and {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Tenth.<br>• A second user, {User-B}, without Settings → Finance, works in {Location-B}. / For example: {User-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S7-04 Writer on the Service Advisor role.<br>• If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done". |
